Company profile 
Founded in 1999, Hon Precision is the global leader in semiconductor test handlers and active thermal 
control (ATC) systems. The company dominates the global AI/HPC final test handler market, supported 
by its advanced thermal management technologies. With over 70% market share in AI/HPC test 
handling, Hon Precision benefits from growing demand for AI, HPC and custom ASIC applications. Its 
solutions are widely adopted by leading OSATs and foundries worldwide, making it a key enabler of 
advanced semiconductor testing.

### Page 6

6

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Growth Catalysts 
Multi-factor dollar content stacking 
We believe the primary growth catalyst for Hon Precision over our forecast horizon stems 
from multi-layered dollar content stacking, where the rise in machine unit value should 
outpace volume growth. Next-gen AI accelerators have historically exhibited more than 50-
70% increase in thermal design power (TDP) per generation, with platforms like Nvidia 
Rubin and Rubin Ultra surging to 2.3kW and 3.6kW vs. Blackwell’s 1.2kW to 1.4kW. 
Managing these extreme thermal loads makes high-end active thermal control (ATC) a 
necessity, driving a structural step-up in ASPs from standard handlers at approximately 
TWD6m to dual-temperature ATC systems at TWD12-15m, and tri-temperature ATC 
systems at TWD15-18m.

Concurrently, as the package size of next-generation ASICs/GPUs increases from the 
current 120mm tray specification to around 150mm, Hon Precision is introducing mega-
tray large-package solutions in October 2026, which command a 25-30% ASP premium 
over mainstream GPU handlers. As the majority of next-generation ASIC/GPU is set to 
exceed the boundary of 120mm, we estimate shipment penetration for mega-tray solutions 
will likely reach 40% in 2027E. Integrated functions of Automated Optical Inspection (AOI) 
will contribute an additional 10-15% ASP uplift to unit value of handler. Furthermore, this 
content expansion extends into the flagship mobile AP market as smartphone processors 
transition to multi-die packaging. For example, Apple’s A20 Pro processor has already 
adopted wafer-level multi-chip module (WMCM) packaging in 2026, requiring high-end ATC 
systems capable of managing up to 3,000W thermal dissipation to resolve device self-
heating.

Hon Precision: ASP multipliers

Source: Daiwa research

High-margin cold plate consumables  
In addition to capital equipment set sales, Hon Precision’s cold plate consumables 
business serves as a crucial recurring revenue engine and a primary driver of gross margin 
expansion. Cold plates carry the highest gross margin across all company product lines. 
Driven by the compression of AI processor lifecycles to 1-1.5 years (down from 2-3 years 
historically), each test handler requires 5-10 distinct, custom-engineered cold plates 
tailored to specific die footprints, socket layouts, and thermal hot-spot geometries. To 
conduct parallel test and maintain the throughput of chip testing, test handler for system 
level test (SLT) requires even more cold plates, which can go up to 24 plates. As a result, 
cold plate tooling generates high-frequency annuity-like sales that recur with every new 
chip platform iteration, independent of major equipment replacements.

Hon Precision: cold plate revenue trend

Source: Company, Daiwa research

Testing duration and volume multiples 
We believe the rapid rise in AI chip complexity is driving structural growth in semiconductor 
testing intensity, positioning Hon Precision as a key global beneficiary through its 
leadership in high-parallelism test handlers. Driven by the mainstream adoption of 
heterogeneous integration (such as TSMC’s CoWoS and Intel’s EMIB), verifying complex 
AI silicon has extended test durations by 2x to 10x vs. standard consumer chips (5 to 10 
seconds). Final test durations for AI GPUs have expanded from hundreds of seconds to 
over 10 minutes per chip. To maintain daily manufacturing throughput despite this 10x 
spike, chipmakers must scale up massive parallel testing architectures and procure 
significantly more handlers, cementing Hon Precision as the primary test handler partner 
for tier-one US AI GPU and CPU players.

Concurrently, escalating chip complexity, TDP, and die costs make field return (RMA) 
penalties far exceed testing expenses, forcing cloud service providers (CSPs) and 
automotive OEMs to mandate system-level testing (SLT). Because SLT executes over 
extended durations ranging from minutes to hours per chip, high-volume production 
mandates automated parallel SLT handlers equipped with high-capacity ATC. Holding 
c.50% market share in AI/HPC SLT handlers alongside Chroma, Hon Precision is 
expanding beyond GPUs into custom CSP ASICs (eg, Google TPU and AWS) and server 
CPUs. As SLT adoption broadens across non-GPU devices, Hon Precision leverages this 
multi-year runway to transition into a full-spectrum semiconductor testing platform.

Global: growing chip complexity – testing time by AI/HPC chip

Source: Daiwa research

CPO unlocks new TAM beyond GPUs 
Beyond conventional electrical testing, Co-Packaged Optics (CPO) represents a multi-year 
paradigm shift towards a multimodal optical-electrical testing platform. Hon Precision 
currently monetises initial CPO deployments by supplying Final Test (FT) handlers for pure 
electrical testing (E/E test) of switch ASICs and Optical Engines (OEs) on production 
platforms such as Nvidia’s Spectrum-X and Quantum-X.

### Page 8

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026

However, the strategic growth catalyst is anchored in next-generation Insertion 4 (package-
level optical and electrical co-testing, O/E co-test). Because an undetected defect in a 
single optical component would force manufacturers to scrap an entire high-value CPO 
module, early and rigorous package-level validation is imperative for yield protection. 
Collaborating directly with ATE leader Teradyne and optical instrument specialists (such as 
Keysight), Hon Precision is developing a specialised ATC handler capable of simultaneous 
optical and electrical validation, including Bit Error Rate (BER), optical loopback, crosstalk, 
and high-power thermal management testing for integrated ASIC and Optical Engine 
modules in a single touchdown.

Engaged with Nvidia CPO switch programmes and actively expanding to additional ASIC 
clients, Hon Precision targets engineering verification in late-2026 and initial shipments in 
2027, ahead of 2028 volume production. Although we expect revenue contribution will 
likely be relatively low (<5% in 2027-2028), it drives valuation and market expectation to 
serve larger total addressable market (TAM).

Market Analysis 
Global testing equipment market 
According to TechInsights, global testing equipment capex is projected to expand 
significantly from USD8.3bn in 2024 to USD26.1bn by 2027E, representing a 46.4% 
CAGR. Notably, while SoC test systems front-loaded strong growth in 2024-25, before 
tapering to 9.6% YoY in 2027E on TechInsights estimates, package handling equipment is 
forecasted to exhibit strong structural resilience and longevity. Based on TechInsights, 
market capex for package handling equipment will likely more than double from USD941m 
in 2024 to USD2.6bn by 2027E (40.9% CAGR), re-accelerating to 46% YoY growth in 
2027E from 34% YoY in 2025. This cadence reflects the phased deployment of test 
infrastructure – initial tester investments are followed by high-volume back-end handling 
installations as advanced packaging (eg, CoWoS, Chiplets) scales into mass production.

Global semi testing equipment: capex  Global semi testing equipment: handler and prober growth

Source: T echInsights, Daiwa research  Source: T echInsights, Daiwa research

Handler market players 
Hon Precision’s overall share in the global test handler TAM rose from 35% in 2024 to 57% 
in 2025, and we forecast this to reach 75% in 2026E and 79% by 2027E. Within the high-
value AI and HPC Final Test (FT) handler segment, Hon Precision maintains a dominant 
market share, exceeding 70% in both sales value and shipment volume, per management. 
In the rapidly expanding AI and HPC SLT handler segment, Hon Precision holds c.50% 
market share in terms of revenue, per management, alongside its primary peer, Chroma, 
establishing a duopoly in global high-end SLT testing.

Hon Precision: handler market share forecasts

Source: Daiwa research

Outside of AI and HPC logic testing, the broader test handler landscape comprises global 
and regional vendors, including Cohu (COHU US, NR), Advantest (6857 JP, JPY28,375, 
Outperform [2]; covered by Daiwa analyst Takeshi Fukuyama), and mainland Chinese 
suppliers such as Changchuan Technology (300604 CH, NR) and JHT Design (603061 
CH, NR).

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026

While global incumbents rely on brand prestige, standard architectures, and bundled 
offerings, Hon Precision excels in leading-edge AI/HPC testing through its ultra-high-power 
Active Thermal Control (ATC) and rapid engineering turnaround. As multi-kilowatt dynamic 
loads (1.5kW–3kW+) and extreme warpage on >120mm packages push standard handlers 
beyond their limits, Hon Precision’s hyper-customised ATC fluidics, high-force micro-
mechanics, and modular designs deliver superior millisecond thermal clamping and agile 
package adaptation. Furthermore, while giants like Advantest dominate the SoC ATE tester 
market (>55% share), their in-house handlers focus primarily on memory and standard 
logic. Consequently, tier-one chipmakers and OSATs standardly decouple tester and 
handler sourcing, thus pairing Advantest or Teradyne testers with Hon Precision’s high-
power ATC handlers. Located directly within Taiwan’s advanced packaging cluster, Hon 
Precision provides real-time, on-site co-validation and custom cold-plate engineering that 
preserve formidable entry barriers against bundled solutions.

Meanwhile, although Chinese competitors have expanded aggressively in domestic mature 
nodes and conventional Pick-and-Place handlers, Hon Precision commands a decisive 
generational lead in high-complexity advanced packaging applications. Chinese vendors 
remain largely confined to lower-wattage consumer, automotive, and industrial IC testing, 
lacking the proven patents, structural rigidity, and micro-level force balancing required to 
safely apply hundreds of kilograms of contact force onto fragile, multi-chip CoWoS 
packages without cracking dies or warping substrates. Moreover, because enterprise AI 
chips cost tens of thousands of dollars each, tier-1 global fabless leaders and North 
American cloud service providers (CSPs) maintain virtually zero tolerance for testing 
failure, establishing an entrenched validation moat for Hon Precision. Free from 
geopolitical export constraints, Hon Precision can seamlessly deploy across global OSAT 
networks in Taiwan, North America, and Southeast Asia.

Balance sheet and cash flow 
Driven by surging AI/HPC testing demand, Hon Precision is actively expanding its capacity 
across the new Desheng facility, localised production at the Changshu site, and 
debottlenecking of headquarter efficiency. The company targets 40-50% capacity growth in 
2026 and another 50% increase in 2027. The expansion of manufacturing space and 
production facilities requires an increase in capital expenditure, which we project Hon 
Precision will ramp up to approximately TWD2.8bn in 2026E, TWD3.8bn in 2027E, and 
TWD4.6bn in 2028E. The company’s operating cash inflows are more than sufficient to 
fully fund factory expansions alongside a c.70% dividend payout ratio.

Hon Precision continues to maintain an un-levered balance sheet with zero financial bank 
debt. Cash and cash equivalents exceeded TWD53bn in 2025; we project this figure to 
surpass TWD75bn by 2028E. Its robust balance sheet underpins peer-leading shareholder 
returns, with ROE to expand from 34.5% in 2025 to 59.6% by 2028E. This self-funding 
cash engine supports management’s targeted c.70% dividend payout ratio.

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Valuation and recommendation 
Initiating coverage with a Buy (1) rating 
We initiate coverage of Hon Precision with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD6,365, 
based on a target PER of 30x applied to our 2027E EPS forecast. When the stock rallied 
above TWD8,000 in 1H26, its forward PER multiple stretched to a cycle peak of 45-50x. 
However, we expect key growth catalysts, including a 50% aggregate capacity expansion, 
alongside higher-ASP shipments of large-form-factor mega tray handlers and co-packaged 
optics (CPO) test solutions, to drive its top line, primarily in 2H27. With existing production 
lines operating at full utilisation, investors are concerned over a potential temporary 
deceleration in growth momentum through 2H26-1H27, ahead of the next potential major 
capacity inflection in 2H27.

However, as the calendar rolls into 2027, we believe market attention will swiftly refocus on 
the company’s multi-year structural thesis. Easing capacity bottlenecks, combined with 
robust ASIC-driven adoption of mega tray architectures and ramping CPO test platforms, 
should spark a powerful volume-and-ASP expansion cycle. The stock is currently trading at 
a 19x 1-year-forward PER vs. its earnings CAGR of 55% over 2026-28E, translating into a 
0.35x PEG ratio. We believe the valuation now heavily discounts the near-term pause in 
revenue and macro noises, presenting a compelling risk-reward profile for long-term 
investors.

Risks to our call 
1. Slower-than-expected AI/HPC investment: A slowdown in AI infrastructure spending 
or delays in GPU, ASIC and server CPU ramps could reduce demand for high-end 
FT/SLT handlers and thermal-control solutions, weakening order growth and revenue 
visibility. 
2. Slower-than-expected new product adoption: Customer qualification delays or 
slower adoption of mega-tray handlers, AOI inspection systems and CPO FT solutions 
could delay product ramps, resulting in revenue growth below our expectations. 
3. Potential share loss: Competitors’ advances in thermal control, customer qualification 
of alternative suppliers or delays in meeting new testing requirements could weaken 
Hon Precision’s position on next-generation platforms, affecting market share and 
pricing power.

### Page 15

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Appendix 
Company introduction 
Hon Precision was founded in Taichung in 2015, building on the technology and industry 
experience of Hon Technology, which was established in 1999. The company develops 
semiconductor test handlers and thermal-control solutions for AI GPUs, AI ASICs, server 
CPUs, automotive chips and consumer electronics, supporting customers from new-
product introduction (NPI) through volume production.

Hon Precision has two main product categories:

• Semiconductor test handlers: Final-test (FT) and system-level-test (SLT) handlers 
automate chip loading, positioning and sorting. These platforms can incorporate active 
thermal control (ATC) systems to maintain chips at specified temperatures during 
testing. 
• Testing fixtures and modules: Mainly customised cold plates and socket layout kits 
(SLKs), which provide thermal management and mechanical interfaces for different chip 
packages. Customers may require replacement cold plates and kits as package designs 
change, even when retaining existing handlers.

Hon Precision’s order mix is significantly weighted towards AI/HPC/ASIC applications, 
which accounted for 78% of orders in 1Q-3Q26, up from 72% in 2025. These applications 
include AI computing, servers and networking. Automotive represented 9% of orders, 
followed by mobile AP and communications at 7%, consumer electronics at 4%, and 
memory and MEMS at 2%.

As for capacity, Hon Precision’s manufacturing footprint is primarily based in Taiwan, with 
Taichung serving as the group’s main R&D, engineering, quality management, and 
equipment production hub. In addition, the company operates a Suzhou facility focused on 
manufacturing cold plates and related kits, while Taichung Plant 4 is expected to provide 
incremental production capacity to support future growth.

Shareholder structure 
Hon Precision’s major shareholders include several investment and development 
companies. As of 17 April 2026, Hung Min Development was the largest shareholder with a 
17.37% stake, followed by Hongji Investment at 12.08% and Hong Yu Development at 
7.80%. Chairman Wen-Ta Hsieh also directly held 1.77%. International institutional 
shareholders included Norges Bank and GIC, with disclosed stakes of 1.30% and 0.97%, 
respectively. The company has a free float of 48%, allowing broad public-market 
participation and supporting trading liquidity.

### Page 17

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Appendix 
What is handler? 
A semiconductor test handler automates the movement, positioning and temperature 
conditioning of packaged chips during testing. It places each device into a test socket, 
applies the required contact force and sorts the device according to the test results. The 
tester performs the electrical measurements, while the handler ensures consistent 
mechanical and thermal conditions.

The process begins with packaged chips loaded into the handler in trays. After temperature 
conditioning, the handler transfers each chip into a test socket connected to the tester 
through a load board. Once electrical contact is established, the tester applies signals and 
measures the chip’s response. For ATC-equipped systems, a customised cold plate works 
with the active thermal control system to maintain the required chip temperature 
throughout testing. The handler then unloads and sorts the chips based on their test 
results.

Semiconductor test handler: workflow and thermal control system

Source: Hon Precision, Daiwa research

Handlers are used in both final test (FT) and system-level test (SLT). FT handlers work 
with automated test equipment (ATE) to check electrical characteristics and functionality 
against specifications. SLT handlers place chips into test positions connected to system-
level platforms, typically comprising test boards, test PCs and power supplies, to evaluate 
operation under conditions resembling actual use.

### Page 18

18

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Semiconductor back-end testing FT workflow

Source: Semiconductor engineering

Thermal control becomes increasingly important as AI/HPC chips consume more power 
and adopt larger, more complex packages. Different test patterns change power 
consumption and heat distribution, requiring dynamic heating or cooling to keep the chip 
within a specified temperature range. Precise handling and temperature control support 
repeatable measurements, while parallel testing and efficient chip movement help improve 
overall test throughput.

Earnings revision 
We revise down our 2026 earnings estimates by 8% to factor in 3Q26 results (we revise 
up our 2026E revenue estimates by 1%). We revise down 2027/28E earnings estimates 
by 9%/8% to reflect product mix with higher contribution from the server business (we 
revise up our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 6/6%). We now expect the GM expansion 
to be slower due to the higher server business contribution that is margin dilutive, thus 
revise down GPM by 0.2/0.2/0.2ppt in 2026/27/28E.

### Page 3

Investment thesis and Valuation & Risks

Inventec is an ODM company focusing on the design and manufacturing of PCs, servers, 
and consumer electronics. While we like Inventec’s solid position in the AI server ODM 
market, we are Neutral rated as we believe valuation already reflects near-term 
positives. We model Inventec earnings growth in 2026-28E to be driven by the 
commercial PC demand recovery and AI server ramp-up. We model AI servers to 
contribute more in 2026-28E. We see P/E valuation as fair at the current level compared 
to its peers and already reflects the solid growth in 2026-28E. Catalysts for further 
upside/downside would be its customers’ demand on AI servers and general server 
demand outlook.

Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$56 is based on 14.2x 2027E P/E, which is 
the company’s average P/E for the past 10 years. 
Key upside/downside risks: (1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
(2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and (3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

jpp-KY(5284 TT)  Trading Buy

2027 年營運多點開花。  
投資建議 ：雖 jpp-KY 2026 年面臨客戶規格調整，使得伺服器
機櫃、機構件等產品短期出貨較少的問題，但考量 2027 年航太
部門營運增速有望提升、伺服器部門在多款產品貢獻下，營運
仍能維持一定增速，使得 jpp-KY 2027 年營運多點開花，故給
予 jpp-KY Trading Buy 的投資建議。  
3Q26 雖有客戶規格調整因素、但電源機構件持續出貨，公告財
報表現佳： 3Q26 因客戶規格調整，使得伺服器機櫃、機構件等
產品短期出貨較少，但整體仍受惠電源櫃結構件出貨，營運維
持高檔，使得 jpp-KY 於 11/06/2026 公告的 3Q26 財報表現佳，
3Q26 營收 11.64 億元， QoQ-3.26% ，稅後純益 2.93 億元，
QoQ+25.62% ， EPS 5.31 元。  
2026 年雖有客戶規格調整問題，但電源櫃產品出貨、歐洲子公
司整頓將至，營運維持高檔： 2026 年 jpp 雖面臨客戶規格調整，
使得伺服器機櫃、機構件等產品短期出貨較少，但電源櫃結構
件開始出貨，加上先前併購的第四家法國子公司 Segnere 整頓
預計於 4Q26 進入尾聲，開始提升 jpp 的航太部門營運增速，使
得 jpp 2026 年營運維持高檔，預估 2026 年營收 46.81 億元，
YoY+25.46% ，稅後純益 9.24 億元，YoY+50.42% ，EPS 16.73 元。 
2027 年航太、伺服器等部門業務推動，營運多點開花： 2027 年
隨著歐洲航太營運部門開始成長，加上 2024 年與航太製造商 -
Safran 的合作仍持續進行，研究部參考過往航太產業的產品開
發時程至少需 2-3 年，預期 2027 年起 Safran 等先前開發的產品
將逐步放量，推升 jpp 的航太部門營運增速。而在伺服器部門，
研究部認為規格調整等下游客戶的生產問題終將結束，在客戶
的生產問題結束後，加上 jpp 近年持續於伺服器產品的開發，
預期 jpp 2027 年的伺服器部門營運仍維持一定增速。綜合以上，
考量 jpp 2027 年有望呈現多產品、多部門的營運多點開花，預
估 2027 年營收 56.27 億元， YoY+20.21% ，稅後純益 11.13 億
元， YoY+20.56% ， EPS 20.17 元。

2 群益投顧

jpp-KY ESG ：  
jpp-KY 依循 AA1000SES 利害關係人議合原則、 GRI 準則編列 2025 年永
續報告書，並於 2024 年設置永續發展委員會，透過定期會議的召開，訂定
年度永續發展政策及與責任商業行為相關的承諾政策、並檢視各項永續發
展議。 jpp-KY 透過安裝太陽能光電系統、節能設備，降低企業運營對環境
的影響，而經  ISO 14064-1: 2018 標準盤查的 2025 年溫室氣體排放量為
7,008.73 公噸  CO2e ，較 2024 年增加 12.61% ；溫室氣體排放強度為 1.32 ，
較 2024 年減少 49.43% 。此外， jpp-KY 認為企業的永續發展不僅僅是環保
層面的努力，也包括對員工與社會的承諾，所以 jpp-KY 重視員工的專業成
長與職涯發展，除了提供員工職能與過渡協助的教育訓練方案之外，也透過
定期績效評核制度，提供明確的回饋與發展建議，作為員工能力精進與升遷
發展的參考依據，同時透過建教合作計畫、舉辦全國性自動化比賽，與社會
建立起良性互動。 jpp-KY 相信透過環保、員工福祉與社會責任方面的制度
化建立過程，將所有利害關係人納入企業長期行動、形成企業文化，過程中
不僅提升品牌形象，還能增強員工的忠誠度與使命感，也能為子孫後代創造
一個更可持續的世界。  
公司簡介：  
jpp-KY ( 經寶精密 )成立於 1995 年，專精於航太、通訊 (伺服器、自動販賣
機 )、醫療顯示等利基型板金市場。公司先前為了降低疫情對航太生產衝擊，
除了跨入航太 MRO 的維修市場，並且爭取到空中巴士的客機改貨機訂單，
也積極發展非航太業務，例如 :AI 伺服器、自動販賣機、新能源機車等。 2Q26
產品營收占比，航太 25% 、通訊 65% 、電子 5%、其他 5%。

4Q26 雖然電源櫃結構件仍會持續出貨，但研究部考量下游客戶電源產品生
產情形與歐美聖誕假期的航太淡季將至，預期 jpp 4Q26 營運仍維持高檔，

貨較少，但電源櫃結構件開始出貨，加上先前併購的第四家法國子公司

資料來源： jpp-KY 。  
2027 年航太、伺服器等部門業務推動，營運多點開花：  
2027 年隨著歐洲航太營運部門開始成長，加上 2024 年與航太製造商 -Safran
的合作仍持續進行，研究部參考過往航太產業的產品開發時程至少需 2-3
年，預期 2027 年起 Safran 等先前開發的產品將逐步放量，推升 jpp 的航太
部門營運增速。而在伺服器部門，研究部認為規格調整等下游客戶的生產問
題終將結束，在客戶的生產問題結束後，加上 jpp 近年持續於伺服器產品的
開發，預期 jpp 2027 年的伺服器部門營運仍維持一定增速。綜合以上，考量
jpp 2027 年有望呈現多產品 、 多部門的營運多點開花 ， 預估2027 年營收 56.27
億元， YoY+20.21% ，稅後純益 11.13 億元， YoY+20.56% ， EPS 20.17 元。  
投資建議：  
雖 jpp-KY 2026 年面臨客戶規格調整，使得伺服器機櫃、機構件等產品短期
出貨較少的問題，但考量 2027 年航太部門營運增速有望提升、伺服器部門
在多款產品貢獻下，營運仍能維持一定增速，使得 jpp-KY 2027 年營運多點
開花，故給予 jpp-KY Trading Buy 的投資建議。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Company Brief 
자동차/모빌리티 
Analyst 김준성 
joonsung.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 배현서 
hyeonseo.bae@meritz.co.kr 배당은 지켜질 것  
3Q26 지배주주 순이익, 컨센서스 -38% 하회 
매출 31.6조원 (+10% YoY) 으로 컨센서스에 부합했다. 출고 판매 (+10% YoY), 소
매 판매 (+8% YoY) 모두 높은 성장을 기록하며, 외형 확대에 기여했다. 
영업이익 1.82조원 (+24% YoY) 으로 컨센서스를 -25% 하회했다. 전년 동기 대미 
관세 25% 부과에 따른 기저효과 +5,330억원 (2025년 11월부터 15%로 하향)이 
존재했고 기말 환율 (원/달러 -12% QoQ 강세) 변동에 따른 판매보증 충당부채 환
입 +6,700억원을 인식했음에도 불구하고, 신규 출시 저가 BEV 모델들의 판매 확대
에 따른 전사 평균 판매가격 하락  할인 경쟁 심화  원재료 및 운송비 상승으로 부
진한 실적을 기록했다. 
지배주주 순이익 1.29조원 (-10% YoY) 으로 컨센서스를 -38% 하회했다. 영업 실적 
부진과 함께 원화 강세에 의한 외화자산 평가손실 및 관계사 실적 악화에 따른 지분
법 이익 감소 (-36% YoY)가 더해진 결과다.

6,000원 이상 배당 유지 전망 
4Q26 이후 실적 방향도 먹구름이 가득하다. 원/달러 환율의 YoY 강세 반영이 시작
된다. 미국 시장 내 경쟁력 확보를 위해 가장 중요한 원/엔 환율의 방향성 또한 비우
호적 흐름이 지속 중이다. 내수 시장을 포함해 전세계 주요 시장 내 중국산 차량과의 
경쟁 강도도 높아지고 있다. 원유 가격 뿐만 아니라 알루미늄, 구리 등 핵심 원재료 
가격의 상승 반영도 이어지고 있다. 
2026년 연간 지배주주 순이익 6.9조원 (-9% YoY) 을 전망한다. 주주환원 총액은 축
소될 것이다. 다만 자사주 매입을 줄이더라도 배당은 전년 수준을 지킬 것으로 기대
한다. 참고로 2025년 실적에 기반한 주주환원은 자사주 매입 0.9조원, 배당 2.6조원 
(주당 6,800원)이었으며, 주주환원율은 48%였다. 현대차그룹은 Boston Dynamics
에 대한 증자를 이어가고 있다. 정의선 회장도 마찬가지다. 그리고 기아의 배당은 현
대차  현대글로비스의 배당과 함께 증자 재원으로 활용되고 있다. 6,000 원 이상의 
배당 유지를 기대하며, 6% 이상 시가 배당 수익률 제시를 통해 주가 하방 경직성 확
보가 가능하다고 판단한다. 기업가치 상승 재개를 위해서는 기존 차량 제조  판매 사
업을 넘어 피지컬 데이터 디바이스 제공 사업 진출 가시성 확보가 필요하다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 11. 2

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 150,000원 
현재주가 (10.30) 95,600원

Document 254: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
미국 록빌·5공장 가세로 3분기 기대치 부합 전망 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 연결 매출액은 1조 3,637억 원(+8.4% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,092억 원(-3.8% YoY, 영업이익률 44.7%)으로 시장 기대치에 부합할 
전망이다. 5월 파업에 따른 생산 차질에도 1~4공장(총 60.4만 리터)의 풀가동과 
신규 공장의 매출 기여가 외형 성장을 뒷받침할 것으로 예상한다. 미국 록빌 
공장(6만 리터)은 3분기부터 매출 기여가 본격화되고, 5공장(18만 리터)도 
인증용(PPQ) 배치 생산을 통해 매출이 확대될 전망이다. 2분기에는 미국 공장·5공장 
관련 비용이 선반영됐으나, 3분기에는 매출 증가에 따른 고정비 부담 완화로 
영업이익률이 전분기 대비 0.3%p 상승할 것으로 예상한다. 
항체 중심 CDMO에서 펩타이드로 확장 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 신규 수주는 약 1.2조 원으로 전년 수주액의 약 20% 
수준에 그치고 있으나, 연말부터 내년까지 수주 모멘텀은 점차 강화될 것으로 
판단한다. 2025년 하반기 이후 미국의 의약품 관세 불확실성이 완화되며 글로벌 
제약사와의 논의가 늘어나고 있어, 신규 수주 계약으로 이어질 것으로 기대된다. 
11월 유상증자 납입이 마무리되면 6공장(18만 리터) 착공과 스위스 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 본격화되며 중장기 성장 기대감도 높아질 전망이다. 특히 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 연내 완료되면 2027년부터 연결 실적에 반영되는 동시에, 항체 의약품 
중심의 CDMO 사업을 GLP-1 등 펩타이드로 확장하는 계기가 될 전망이다. 
목표주가 200만 원으로 하향, 수주 회복이 관건 
삼성바이오로직스의 목표주가를 209만 원에서 200만 원으로 하향한다. 유상증자 
신주 발행에 따른 주당가치 4.7% 희석 효과를 반영했다. 폴리펩타이드 인수 효과는 
거래완료 이후 실적 추정치에 반영할 예정이다. 노조 파업 불확실성이 주가에 
부담으로 작용할 수 있어 신규 수주 모멘텀의 회복이 필요한 시점이다. 
목표주가 (하향) 2,000,000원

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

《⼤空头》原型 Steve Eisman 强调，与 AI 相关资产占标普 500 逾 65% 权重。他拆解四种市
场前景，认为有 70% 概率出现回撤或熊市。五⼤核⼼⻛险涵盖：云巨头被迫 " 重资产化 " 、电
⼒与审批的物理瓶颈、极端客⼾集中度、 OpenAI 财务隐患，以及最关键的，模型间⼏乎为
零的转换成本引发价格战。
摘要：
当⾼达 8500 亿美元的 AI 资本开⽀⾼度系于极少数缺乏⻓期护城河的模型公司时，华尔街传奇空
头警告，⼀旦现实不及预期，不仅市场将⾯临⼤规模回调，美国经济也极易被推⼊衰退。
11 ⽉ 9 ⽇，《⼤空头》原型之⼀、前 Neuberger Berman 基⾦经理 Steve Eisman 在播客《 The
Real Eisman Playbook 》上线了⼀期 40 分钟的单⼈播客。
⾯对当下占据绝对主导地位的 AI 投资狂潮， Steve Eisman 并未质疑 AI 改变世界的潜⼒，但他将
审视的⽬光投向了商业本质：巨⼤的资本⽀出由谁买单？增⻓是否可持续？以及最终谁能真正

获利？
Steve Eisman 在开场直击核⼼：
节⽬上线前四天，美国中期选举⺠主党拿下参众两院。 11 ⽉ 5 ⽇标普 500 跌 4.4% 、纳指跌 5.5% ，
电⼒、芯⽚与数据中⼼设备个股普遍跌 7% 到 17% 。 11 ⽉ 8 ⽇英伟达董事会宣布对⻩仁勋启动独
⽴调查，本周两⽇股价累跌 10% 。
⾯对今年单是超⼤规模云⼚商就⾼达 8500 亿美元的 AI 基础设施资本开⽀， Steve Eisman 围绕产
业链的偿付关系，拆解了四种市场情景与五⼤核⼼⻛险。
65% 的标普权重与 70% 的回撤概率
Eisman ⾸先指出， AI 早已不仅是科技板块的主题，⽽是整个市场的定价变量。他算了⼀笔账：
AI 已经成为当今投资界的主导主题。它驱动着股市，影响着债市，并在很⼤程度上驱动着整个美
国经济。美国很容易陷⼊衰退，我们⼏乎肯定还会看到⼀次⼤规模的市场回调。

综合来看， Eisman 认为有 70% 的概率市场将⾯临某种形式的回撤或熊市。
巨头被迫 “ 重资产化 ” ，表外融资幽灵重现
在具体的⻛险拆解中， Eisman 将第⼀⼤⻛险指向了出钱⽅ —— 超⼤规模云⼚商的资产负债表。
他认为：
信息技术板块占标普 500 指数 40% ，加上⾕歌、亚⻢逊等 “ 科技属性 ” 公司，⽐例达到 50% ；
再算上今年为 AI 投⼊ 2500 亿美元的公⽤事业板块，占 3% ，以及⼯业板块中建设数据中⼼和供
电的公司，如伊顿、 GE Vernova ；
最后加上为 AI 融资的⾦融机构。
与 AI 相关的权重远远超过标普 500 的 65% 。
据我判断，完全不涉及 AI 的只有两个板块，必需消费品和医疗保健，两者加起来只占标普的
14% 。
整个市场不仅直接或间接地和 AI 相关，市场⾛向取决于 AI 未来的健康和成功。

⽜市情景（ 30% 概率）：  AI 持续改变世界，模型公司和英伟达不断推⾼股市。
熊市情景（ 30% 概率）：  采⽤率缓慢，价格战导致回报不及预期，最终引发全⾯衰退、市场
崩盘及私募信贷市场平仓。
中间情景（ 40% 概率）：  数据中⼼建设远超需求。他引⽤ PAA Research 分析师的估算，到
2027 年底美国数据中⼼产能将达到需求的两倍，导致资本开⽀在 2028 年放缓。 “ 结果是市场
回吐⼤部分 AI 涨幅，并出现⼀段时间的熊市。 ”
末⽇情景（ 0% 概率）：  对于机器接管地球的科幻叙事，他⼀针⻅⾎地指出： “他们兜售这套说
法，顺着⾃⼰制造出来的恐惧情绪⾛，⽬的是吓住政府，⽤监管把竞争清出市场。”
AI 改变了游戏规则。归根结底，AI 资本开⽀的需求把超⼤规模⼚商的商业模式从轻资本改成了重
资本。

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook
No
vember 2, 2026
All data, projections and opinions are as of the date of this report and subject to change.
IN THIS ISSUE
Macro Strategy—Guide to El Niño: What It Is, Why It Matters, and What to Do
About It: As crude oil flirts with April’s highs, and the price of diesel sits comfortably
above $6 per gallon, disruption to the global energy complex has emerged as a major
theme of 2026. Add yet another variable to the equation: the current “El Niño” event,

on track to be one of the strongest on record. The upshot: more extreme weather
from droughts to heavy rains, warmer temperatures globally, lower crop production in
commodity-exporting economies, and higher prices across major agricultural products.
Importantly, these pressures won’t be equally distributed and will largely depend on the
magnitude and timing of weather events. Among the most vulnerable will be heavily
import-dependent countries in the Middle East, Africa and parts of Asia, while developed

economies that count food as a smaller share of consumer spending should be better
insulated. For investors, the prospect of a very strong El Niño only further reinforces our
conviction in several structural trends including a return to higher inflation and higher
interest rates, elevated commodity prices amid heightened geopolitical risk and supply
chain shortages, and greater investment in energy infrastructure and supply chain
resilience. In portfolios, we favor commodities as both an inflation hedge and diversifier.

Market View—Another Tale of Market Rotation: October offered another reminder
that headline index returns do not always tell the full story. The S&P 500 ended the month
slightly lower, but the more muted headline return masked meaningful shifts in market
leadership beneath the surface. At the center of the volatility was a sharp unwind in the
Artificial Intelligence (AI) trade, particularly across semiconductors and memory stocks. Yet
pressure on AI market leaders has not translated into a broad retreat from U.S. Equities.

Instead, capital has moved toward previously lagging areas of the market, with Value-
oriented, defensive and rate-sensitive stocks gaining ground. While it remains too early to
determine whether the shift will prove durable, October’s rotation may reflect less a retreat
from the AI theme and more a recalibration of valuations and expected returns as market
leadership broadens. For investors, the speed of the shift reinforces the importance of
diversification as the bull market enters a potentially more mature and differentiated phase.

Thought of the Week—Reorienting Around AI: From Corporate Plans to Construction
to Capital Goods: AI is reshaping the physical economy, and companies are reorienting
capacity and investment plans to meet surging demand for data centers, power and
infrastructure. Industrial firms are adding or reviving capacity, while data center construction
has increased more than 60% over the past year and is outpacing other major construction
categories. At the same time, robust capital goods orders and long lead times for inputs such

as generators, switchboards, chillers and transformers highlight both the strength of demand
and the supply constraints the buildout faces. Taking stock of these signposts, our view
is reinforced that the AI investment cycle remains in its middle innings, creating potential
opportunities for companies supplying the equipment and infrastructure behind the buildout.

Ariana Chiu

Strategist
Kirsten Cabacungan

A series of macro shocks, including
higher energy prices, rising Treasury
yields and shifting Federal Reserve
expectations, have tested markets,
but resilient economic growth, strong
corporate earnings and ongoing
artificial intelligence-related investment

continue to support a constructive
outlook for Equities and risk assets.
We remain overweight Equities, favor
U.S. stocks, see potential opportunities
in broader market participation and
select cyclical sectors, and view higher
bond yields as attractive to add income
and extend duration.

### Page 2

2 of 8 November 2, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Guide to El Niño: What It Is, Why It Matters, and What to Do About It
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
As crude oil flirts with April’s highs, and the price of diesel sits comfortably above $6 per
gallon, disruption to the global energy complex has emerged as a major theme of 2026.
Add yet another variable to the equation: the current “El Niño” event, on track to be one

of the strongest on record. Below we answer frequently asked questions on El Niño,
the potential impact on the global economy and markets and, most importantly, how
investors can position portfolios accordingly.
What is El Niño, and what makes this time different? El Niño is the warm phase
of the “El Niño-Southern Oscillation,” a natural climate pattern occurring every two to
seven years. Simply put, El Niño refers to the warming of sea surface temperatures in

the eastern Pacific, historically leading to drier conditions in some areas (Southeast Asia,
Australia, Central America, southern Africa) and wetter conditions in others (southern
U.S., parts of Latin America) (Exhibit 1).
What makes this episode different? Should forecasts prove correct, this El Niño is on
track to be historic. The National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration’s latest
outlook assigns at least an 83% probability that El Niño’s strength during October-

December will exceed that of any previous episode since 1950. Already, its impact has
been felt via wildfires in Indonesia, the weakest monsoon season in India since 2015, 1
and severe flooding from Paraguay to Peru. The upshot: more extreme weather, hotter
temperatures globally, lower crop production in commodity-exporting economies, and
higher prices across major agricultural products.
Exhibit 1: El Niño's Impact Varies Globally.
Geography Typical rainfall pattern Specific impacts

South Asia Weaker summer monsoon risk Lower crop yields, water shortages, food inflation
Southeast Asia Drier Lower rice and palm oil production, wildfire risk, food inflation
United States Wetter in the south; warmer/drier in the north Southern flood risk; lower northern heating demand
Central America & Northern South America Drier Drought, lower crop yields, water shortages, food inflation, lower hydropower output
Southern Africa Drier Weaker maize harvests, food inflation, lower hydropower output

Western South America Wetter along the Pacific coast Flooding, infrastructure damage, disrupted fisheries
Southern South America Wetter Flooding and crop damage; potential yield gains in some areas
Australia Drier Drought, agricultural losses, elevated wildfire risk, food inflation
Source: National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration, World Meteorological Organization, Food and Agriculture Organization, Australian Bureau of Meteorology, World Bank. Data as of

October 30, 2026.
How will El Niño impact the global economy and markets? Which regions will be
most affected? El Niño’s impact on the global economy and markets is likely to be felt
most via commodity price inflation. Alongside higher energy and fertilizer costs resulting
from war in Europe and the Middle East, El Niño could put further upward pressure on
commodities like wheat, sugar, corn, soy, cocoa and coffee as drought (or extreme rain)
weighs on crop yields and production. Latest estimates from the United Nations Food

and Agriculture Organization (FAO) suggest global food prices were up 5.8% year-over-
year (YoY) in September (Exhibit 2). Livestock is also likely to be challenged by higher
temperatures globally, while flooding has already threatened copper production in
Chile. Meanwhile, with hydropower accounting for more than half of the average annual
electricity generation in Brazil, Colombia, Peru and other Latin American countries, 2
below-average rainfall in affected areas has huge implications for electricity generation.

As an example, Colombia’s natural gas demand nearly doubled during the 2023-2024 El
Niño event.2 Another key watchpoint will be whether drier conditions reduce water levels
1 India Meteorological Department, Bloomberg. Refers to rainfall between June and September as of September 30, 2026.
2 International Energy Agency, as of October 7, 2026
Investment Implications
We expect El Niño to place upward
pressure on agricultural goods
prices globally. We continue to

see commodities as a strategic
diversifier and hedge against inflation
in portfolios. The U.S. remains our
preferred region with companies in
Industrials and Materials as potential
beneficiaries. Meanwhile, economic
impacts to the emerging world will vary
by country but the broader Emerging
Market (EM) equity market could be
less exposed given its significant tilt
toward Technology.

### Page 3

3 of 8 November 2, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
in the Panama Canal and other key shipping routes, particularly in light of the Strait of
Hormuz closure.
These pressures won’t be equally distributed and will heavily depend on this El Niño’s
severity. Among the more vulnerable to higher agricultural prices will be heavily import-
dependent countries in the Middle East, Africa, and parts of Asia. So too will economies
that count food as a larger share of consumer spending such as the Philippines (36%),

Indonesia (33%), Egypt (33%), India (28%), and Vietnam (26%) (Exhibit 3). Developed
economies like the U.S. (6%), Canada (10%), and Germany (11%) stand to be better
insulated from a food-related shock to headline inflation.
Within the U.S., some impacts could be positive with a potentially milder winter reducing
heating costs and wetter conditions in the South improving agricultural conditions. A recent
paper from the Federal Reserve Bank of San Francisco found that, in addition to putting

upward pressure on personal consumption expenditures (PCE) inflation, strong El Niño
episodes have been historically associated with a temporary boost to employment in the
following year via better construction conditions in the North and weather-event recovery
efforts.3 In California and parts of the Southwest, El Niño will be yet another stress test for
property insurers as flooding and landslide risks increase.
For more vulnerable developing economies, meanwhile, higher food and energy costs could

present a policy dilemma. On one hand, governments in vulnerable areas may move to
introduce fiscal relief to support households and boost growth. History points to plenty of
examples of this, such as Indonesia’s cash assistance in late 2023 or Thailand’s $1.8 billion
in loans to farmers in 2015. On the other hand, elevated inflation expectations and, in
some cases, weaker currencies could limit EM central banks’ room to ease monetary policy,
particularly with several major developed central banks aligned in hiking interest rates. Also

complicating matters: Global sovereign debt surpassed $110 trillion in Q2 2026, leaving
less fiscal space to absorb incoming shocks. 4
How can investors position portfolios accordingly? In many ways, the prospect of a
very strong El Niño only reinforces structural trends we’ve identified as defining the coming
years: a return to higher inflation and higher interest rates; elevated commodity prices
amid heightened geopolitical risk and resource/supply shortages; and greater investment in

energy infrastructure and supply chain resilience.
Considering these themes, we continue to like commodities not just as a hedge against
higher inflation but also as a diversification tool in portfolios given their negative correlation
with Equities and bonds. That’s not to mention secular forces placing upward pressure on
commodity prices such as the resource-intensive AI buildout and broader protectionism. To
wit, the Bloomberg Commodity Index, which spans oil, gold, various metals and agricultural

goods has gained nearly 30% this year, handily outperforming the S&P 500 (+1%) and the
Bloomberg U.S. Aggregate Bond Index (-1%).
Within Equities, the U.S. is still our preferred region. While upside risk to inflation remains
a risk to watch going into 2027, food is a relatively small share of the consumer basket in
the U.S., and neither strong services spending nor capital investment should be materially
affected by El Niño. Companies within the Industrials and Materials sectors focused on

precision agriculture, water management and crop protection may be positioned to benefit.
Import-dependent EM economies remain particularly exposed, though impacts will vary
widely depending on the magnitude and timing of weather events. It’s worth noting too that
EM Equities remain heavily concentrated in East Asian markets with substantial exposure to
technology, leaving the broader index arguably less exposed to the economic ramifications
of El Niño than in past episodes. Indeed, even after recent weakness in semiconductor

names, Taiwan, Korea, and China together account for a near-70% of the broader MSCI
Emerging Markets Index. 5 We maintain a slight overweight to the region in portfolios given
earnings strength but favor an active approach.

MARKET VIEW
Another Tale of Market Rotation
Kirsten Cabacungan, Vice President and Senior Investment Strategist
October offered another reminder that headline index returns do not always tell the
full story. The S&P 500 ended the month slightly lower, down almost 1%, but the more
muted headline return masked meaningful shifts in market leadership beneath the
surface. At the center of the volatility was a sharp unwind in the AI trade, particularly

across semiconductors and memory stocks, with the Philadelphia Semiconductor Index
experiencing a 19% intramonth drawdown. The dominant concerns in recent months
have centered on the sustainability of AI infrastructure spending and whether the scale
of investment will ultimately generate sufficient returns. Those only intensified amid a
sharp rise in bond yields after the 10-year U.S. Treasury yield spiked above 5.3% as of
October 5 for the first time since 2002, raising scrutiny over AI financing conditions and

whether higher borrowing costs could increase hurdle rates for continued investment
and slow the pace of capital spending.
Yet the pressure on AI market leaders has not translated into a broad retreat from U.S.
Equities. Instead, capital has moved toward previously lagging areas of the market,
with Value-oriented, defensive and rate-sensitive stocks gaining ground. Value defeated
Growth in October, with the Russell 1000 Value Index outperforming its Growth

counterpart by 10 percentage points, an outcome seen in only about 1% of months since
1979. The move extends Value's outperformance for the year, with the index up 23%
year-to-date compared with down almost 1% for Growth. Participation also improved.
The equal-weighted S&P 500 outperformed its market-cap-weighted counterpart by its
widest monthly gap since 2009 (Exhibit 4A), while the share of S&P 500 constituents
trading above their 200-day moving average rebounded to 62% from just 43% at the

end of September, and 76% of stocks outperformed the index for the month.

The latest rotation is not the first attempt at a change in market leadership this year.
The more important question is what that shift represents: a fundamental reassessment
of the AI trade or another step toward broader, more mature market leadership?
Recent Q3 Technology earnings results have sharpened that debate. Investors are
increasingly looking beyond the scale of AI spending to companies’ ability to translate
that investment into durable growth and attractive returns. The result may be greater

dispersion not only between Growth and Value, but also among AI leaders as investors
become more discerning about which companies can justify the scale of their
investments.
Investment Implications
We continue to look for potential
missing opportunities and favor
diversification across and within asset
classes in a multi-asset portfolio.
In particular, we see potential
opportunities to broaden exposure
beyond AI leaders toward areas
where earnings, capital spending and

productivity trends are improving,
including Value, Small- and Mid-caps,
and select non-U.S. markets, particularly
EMs and Japan. As market leadership
evolves, maintaining diversified
exposures and periodically rebalancing
portfolios can help investors participate
in a broader set of possible return
drivers without becoming overly
dependent on any single theme.

### Page 5

5 of 8 November 2, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
But greater selectivity does not necessarily signal the end of the AI investment cycle.
YoY growth rates may become harder to maintain after two years of rapid acceleration
as the comparison base rises, but absolute levels of AI capital spending remain elevated.
What matters more is whether spending translates into strong productivity and earnings
growth ahead and whether AI demand continues to support investment. October’s

volatility may therefore reflect less a retreat from the AI theme and more a recalibration
of valuations and expected returns, as other areas of the market begin to contribute
more meaningfully to market performance.
Several developments ahead will help determine whether that rotation has staying
power. One important test is whether broader participation can persist if pressure on AI
stocks continues. Continued strength outside the AI trade would support the case that

investors are reallocating capital rather than broadly reducing risk. The path of monetary
policy will also matter as investors assess where borrowing costs may be headed. The
Federal Reserve’s not-so-hawkish hold in October offered some reassurance, but the
path ahead remains dependent on the evolving balance between inflation and growth.
Any signs of resolution of geopolitical tensions remain an important swing factor
particularly as recent flashpoints in the Middle East have pressured energy prices higher

again. Results of the midterm elections should also provide greater visibility into the
policy landscape for the next few years. While the market does not need every clearing
event to resolve favorably, greater clarity around these uncertainties could help narrow
the range of potential outcomes facing investors.
For investors, October’s sudden change in leadership reinforces the importance of
diversification. Leadership can shift quickly, leaving portfolios that become overly

dependent on a single theme, sector or style particularly vulnerable when it does. We
remain constructive on U.S. Equities and believe that the market may be transitioning
more toward a mid-cycle phase in which returns become broader but also more
measured compared to the sharp acceleration earlier in the cycle. That argues not for
abandoning the market’s long-term winners, but for complementing them with exposure
to additional sources of returns across style, size, sector and region.

6 of 8 November 2, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
Reorienting around AI: From Corporate Plans to Construction to Capital Goods
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
For all the questions (and recent market weakness) hanging over the AI trade, from
monetization and cash burn to financing risk and overcapacity, a measurable aspect is
the buildout itself. Data center construction is lifting construction spending and capital

goods orders, while companies are adding and reviving capacity to meet AI-related
demand. Still, planning delays, access to power, labor scarcity, local politics and the cost
of capital remain meaningful constraints on the buildout’s pace or profitability.
Corporate Plans. Take Caterpillar as one example that has resumed production of a
10-megawatt natural gas turbine discontinued back in 2022. A 175-year old glassmaker,
Corning, is leaning into optical connectivity as AI clusters require faster connections of

optical fiber cabling between servers. Caterpillar and Corning are just two companies
among others pivoting to supply data centers.
Construction to Capital Goods. The AI reorientation is evident in construction
spending as well, where data center construction has outpaced every other major
residential and nonresidential category. Over the last year AI-related construction on data
centers has surged more than 60% while spending on residential, lodging, commercial

or office has declined notably (Exhibit 5A). Data center construction is running at an $85
billion (B) annualized rate this year, versus roughly $49B a year earlier, and now accounts
for 11% of private nonresidential construction, up from just 2% in 2022 according to
the Census Bureau. Similarly, demand for artificial intelligence business equipment and
transportation equipment drove an increase in durable goods orders in September on the
back of new orders for U.S.-manufactured capital goods, suggesting business spending

on equipment remained robust in Q3. Another factor, see Exhibit 5B, where critical
components such as generators, switchboards, chillers and transformers continue to
face long lead times that affect delivery timelines and ultimate profitability for new data
center development.
These signposts reinforce our view that we are only in the middle innings of the AI
investment cycle. From an investment perspective, huge demand for data centers, power
systems, networking infrastructure, specialized chips and other equipment continues

to strain supply, creating both bottlenecks and potential opportunities for companies
powering the buildout.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500/Market-cap Weighted Index is a market-capitalization-weighted index that is widely regarded as the best single gauge of large-cap U.S. equities. The index includes 500 leading

companies and covers approximately 80% of available market capitalization.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index includes the same constituents as the capitalization weighted S&P 500, but each company in the S&P 500 EWI is allocated a fixed weight - or 0.2% of the
index total at each quarterly rebalance.
Bloomberg U.S. Aggregate Bond Index is a broad-based, market-capitalization-weighted benchmark that measures the performance of the U.S. investment-grade, U.S. dollar-denominated,

fixed-rate taxable bond market
Bloomberg Commodity Index is a broadly diversified financial benchmark that tracks the price movements of physical commodities through futures contracts.
MSCI Emerging Market Index captures large and mid cap representation across 24 Emerging Markets (EM) countries.
United Nations Food and Agriculture Food Price Index is a measure of the monthly change in international prices of a basket of food commodities.
Philadelphia Semiconductor Index is a market-cap-weighted index focusing on 30 leading semiconductor firms, offering insights into the tech market.

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

ADVANCED ECONOMIES
In brief. The week was dominated by large market swings triggered by
the US midterms results. In the end, energy prices end the week much
lower, bond yields somewhat lower and stock prices down in tech-heavy
markets and slightly up elsewhere, notably in Europe. Incoming data
confirmed a picture of resilient growth overall. The Bank of England

hiked its policy rate by 25 bps and signaled it could do more. Market
pressure on France eased somewhat.
EUROZONE
Energy pushes inflation up, but underlying pressures remain rela -
tively contained. Headline inflation rose to 4.0% y/y in October, its
highest in three years, strengthening the case for another ECB rate
increase in December. Energy remained the main driver, while core in-
flation rose by 2.6%. The ECB wage tracker points to negotiated wage

growth slowing to 2.3% by September 2027. Meanwhile, retail sales fell
0.7% m/m in September and the average interest rate on new business
loans increased 13bp to 3.90%.
To watch next week: September trade balance, Q3 GDP second esti -
mate and employment (Friday).
France
EVs boost activity, market pressure eases somewhat. After already
bolstering household consumption in Q3, new car registrations rose
7% y/y in October (+4% y/y YTD), driven by another surge in electric

car sales (+81% y/y in October; +78% y/y YTD). In parallel, manufac -
turing output rebounded by 0.5% m/m in September, supported by
the automotive sector (+7.5% m/m but -5.5% y/y) and capital goods
(+2.6% m/m and +4.8% y/y). However, industrial production fell by
0.5% m/m (and 1.3% y/y), as a result of a decline in electricity produc-
tion (-4.2% m/m). The composite October PMI was revised down by
1.8 points from the flash estimate to 46.6 (-4.5 m/m), dragged down by

services. This sharp downgrade likely reflects the hit to sentiment from
the October 17 riots; however, precedents suggest activity itself should
not be visibly impacted. Appropriately indifferent to the meandering
journey of the draft budget through Parliament, the OAT-bund spread
narrowed to 155 bps (-10 bps in one week) amid a broad-based decline
in government bond yields across advanced economies; Thursday’s
long-term debt auction, the last of the year, was well-subscribed. Stri-

ving to meet its deficit target of 5.4% of GDP in 2026, the government
announced EUR3.8bn of spending cuts to offset EUR3.7bn in additional
expenditure. The High Council for Public Finance nevertheless sees the
deficit reaching 5.5%. The 2027 budget will start being discussed by
the Senate from next week, in its original version, after the national
Assembly unanimously rejected its amended version that would have
led to a 5.9% deficit.
To watch next week: Q3 unemployment rate and the Banque de France

monthly business survey (Tuesday).
Germany
Industrial activity picks up. New orders rebounded 4.4% m/m in Sep -
tember and industrial production increased 0.7%, with the auto sec -
tor delivering particularly striking gains of 10.5% in orders and 17.1%
in production. Mechanical-engineering orders also recovered (+8.3%),
while pharmaceutical production increased by 8.0%. By contrast, large
orders in aerospace, shipbuilding, rail and defence fell by 10.4%, while

machinery and equipment production declined by 6.5%. October's com-
posite PMI was revised up to 53.4, signaling continued strong expan -
sion.
Italy
Services keep the economy above water as manufacturing slips back.
The composite PMI eased to 50.8 in October, its lowest since May.
Manufacturing returned to contraction at 49.4, its weakest reading
since January, while services strengthened to 52.0 and recorded a fifth
consecutive month of growth. Both sectors reported increasing input-

cost and output-price inflation.
UNITED KINGDOM
The BoE hikes and leaves the door open to more. The Bank of England
raised Bank Rate by 25bp to 4%, its first increase since August 2023, in a
more hawkish than expected 8-1 vote, with Catherine Mann favouring
a 50bp hike. The November Monetary Policy Report sees CPI inflation
peaking around 5% y/y by mid-2027 and upgraded Q4 2026 growth to
2.2%, while cutting the 2027 and 2028 growth projections. The com -

posite PMI meanwhile improved to 52.9, its highest since February,
with new business growing at its strongest pace in nine months and
private-sector employment increasing for the first time in 25 months.
To watch next week: Q3 GDP , business investment, industrial produc-
tion (Thursday).
UNITED STATES
Post election market jitters and a hot jobs report. The Democrats’
unexpectedly sweeping victory in the midterms (the largest wave
since 1974) initially led markets to rally on (i) relief that the President

recognised the results, and (ii) hopes of a swift end to the Iran war
(oil prices and bond yields down, stock prices up). But this was soon
followed by a sharp equity sell-off on 5 November, with the S&P 500
down 4.4% and the Nasdaq down 5.0%, erasing USD2.7 Trillion of mar-
ket capitalization in one day. Why? Because of the realization that vo -
ters strongly supported AI-restrictive measures on the ballot (46 out
of 47) and candidates — for Congress and Governor seats — in favour

of curtailing data centers buildout. By the week-end, however, senti -
ment had turned around again, helped in part by a much stronger than
expected jobs report: payrolls increased by 165,000 in October against
a 70,000 consensus, while the previous two months were revised hi -
gher and unemployment edged down to 4.1%. Average hourly earnings
rose 0.4% m/m, their strongest monthly pace since November 2025.
Manufacturing activity also strengthened, with the ISM rising to 55.5,

and the non-manufacturing index easing to a still buoyant 54.5.
To watch next week: October CPI (Tuesday); October PPI (Friday).
JAPAN
Wage growth accelerates. Scheduled contractual earnings increased
3.7% y/y in September and total wage growth accelerated to 4.6%,
helped by strong bonus payments. Real wage growth remained posi -
tive at 2.2% y/y.
EMERGING ECONOMIES
Portfolio outflows. According to IIF data, non-resident portfolio invest-
ment flows recorded USD33.8bn of outflows in October, the largest

monthly outflow since March and up from USD19.3bn in September.
Equities accounted for USD31.7bn, led by USD28.3bn from emerging
Asia, as doubts over AI-investment returns and profit-taking hit North
Asian markets particularly hard. Taiwan and South Korea suffered a
further sharp market correction in the first week of November.

### Page 2

7
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.41 / 9 November 2026

for a changing
world
Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
ASIA
PMI: expanding except for the Philippines. The ASEAN PMI rose to 53.1
in October from 52.1, pointing to faster expansion. India remained ro -
bust despite a slight decline (54.7). In Taiwan and South Korea, the
manufacturing PMIs declined but remained expansionary, at 54.9 and
51.3, respectively. Indonesia’s real GDP growth accelerated to 5.5% y/y

in Q3 2026 from 5.3% in Q2 marking its fastest pace since 2013.
China
The PBOC steps in as equity correction deepens. The central bank in -
creased its use of liquidity tools designed to support equity purchases,
including the special relending facility for share buybacks and stake in-
creases and a third round of the Securities, Funds and Insurance Com-
panies Swap Facility, for which 31 institutions applied for more than
RMB100bn. The measures follow heavy stock-market losses between

June and October. The PBOC also raised the reserve-requirement ratio
on foreign-currency deposits to 6% from 4%, effective 15 November, in
an effort to slow the renminbi's appreciation.
Weak domestic inflation pressure: In October, CPI inflation slowed to
0.7% y/y from 0.9% in September. Fuel prices increased by 20.2% y/y,
food prices fell -2.9% and core inflation slowed to 0.8% y/y from 1.0%.
Producer price inflation continued to rise (reaching 5.7% vs. 5.0% in

September), principally driven by higher energy and electronics prices.
To watch next week: External-trade data (Tuesday).
EMEA
Central Europe
PMIs expansionary except in Romania. Poland’s PMI hit the 50-mark
for the first time since April 2025 thanks to export orders. PMI was
also higher in Czech Rep. (55), supported by new orders, output and
employment. Hungary’s PMI fell marginally to 53.1.
Poland and Czech Republic central banks hold and hike respectively,

as expected.
Gulf
PMIs resilient except Qatar (deteriorating further to 46.3).
Saudi Arabia: Massive budget deficit in sight. Saudi Arabia recorded a
budget deficit of USD22.5bn in Q3, narrowing by 5% y/y. But over the
first nine months of 2026, budget deficit reached USD65.1bn, up from
USD48bn in 2025, despite a rather good revenue performance (+2.8%
total; +7.8% for non-oil revenues; -1.3% for oil revenues). The 8% rise in
spending in Q1-Q3 partly due to the war will make it difficult to meet

the 2026 revised target (deficit of 4.9% of GDP).
LATIN AMERICA
PMIs remain weak across the region, with only Colombia in expansion.
Brazil’s central bank pauses rate cuts, whereas Mexico’s central bank
cuts rates whilst signalling an upcoming pause. On 4 November, Bra -
zil’s central bank held its Selic rate at 13.75%, interrupting a series of
five consecutive cuts, amid rising inflation (4.6% y/y in September, up
from 4.2%), a sharp post-election real depreciation on fiscal concerns,

and higher energy prices. The central bank also revised up its 2027
inflation forecast to 4.7% (from 3.9%) and removed forward guidance
hinting at further cuts. Mexico’s central bank unexpectedly cut its po -
licy rate by 25bp to 6.25% on 5 November. This decision came despite
above-target inflation (3.5% against a 3% target), a peso depreciation
of more than 9% against USD since September, and relatively strong Q3
GDP growth (3.2% y/y). The central bank signaled that rates would now

remain on hold for an extended period.
COMMODITIES
Geopolitical relief delivers a dramatic energy-price reversal. Mar -
kets have priced in the reduced risk of short-term conflict escalation
through a sharp decline in prices. Beyond this, however, they lack di -
rection, wavering between positive signals (the resumption of indirect
negotiations between the belligerents) and less favorable ones (in -
creased vessel damage in the strait). Brent dropped 7.6% over the week

to USD96.8/bbl as markets priced a reduced risk of near-term conflict
escalation. European gas prices fell almost in lockstep, declining 7.7%
to EUR87.6/MWh. US commercial crude inventories continued to de -
cline, although more modestly than over the preceding four weeks.
Diesel crack spread’s reaction to US elections has been much more
limited than for crude. EU gas storage ended the refilling season at 75%
of capacity (vs. 80% goal).
y/y = year-on-year

m/m = month-on-month
bp = basis point
pp = percentage point
mb = million barrels
MBtu = Million British Thermal Units

Document 369: IPO前夕，Anthropic再追加114亿美元算力采购订单

Source PDF: all report pdf/IPO前夕，Anthropic再追加114亿美元算力采购订单.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-11

### Page 1

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
在距离拟定上市⽇仅剩⼀周之际， Anthropic 向 SEC 提交⽂件显⽰，其过去⼀个⽉内向微软
和⾕歌新增约 114 亿美元云计算采购承诺，使两平台承诺⽀出分别升⾄ 492 亿和 1216 亿美
元；同时披露与 Lambda 的 349 亿美元协议因数据中⼼建设延期⾯临取消⻛险。
摘要：
Anthropic 在冲刺美国科技史上最⼤规模 IPO 的关键节点，再度⼤幅扩充云计算采购承诺。
根据 Anthropic 周⼆向美国证券交易委员会（ SEC ）提交的招股⽂件，该公司过去⼀个⽉内与微
软和⾕歌新增约 114 亿美元云服务采购承诺，使其在两家平台上的总⽀出承诺分别升⾄ 492 亿美
元和 1216 亿美元。
与此同时， Anthropic 披露其与⼩型云服务商 Lambda 签订的 349 亿美元租赁协议存在被取消的⻛
险，原因是 Lambda 未能在约定期限内完成数据中⼼建设。
上述披露距Anthropic 计划定价⽇（ 11 ⽉ 18 ⽇，周三）仅剩 7 天。此次 IPO 拟通过公开发⾏与私募

配售合计筹资最⾼ 904 亿美元，若计⼊超额配售权，募资总额将逼近 1000 亿美元，有望刷新
SpaceX 此前创下的 865 亿美元纪录。然⽽， AI 相关股票上周刚经历⼀轮急跌，市场对数据中⼼
监管收紧及成本上升的忧虑尚未消散，此番算⼒采购规模的再度扩张，势必进⼀步加剧投资者
对其盈利前景的审视。
微软、⾕歌承诺⾦额双双上调
根据周⼆⽂件，Anthropic 已承诺在 2033 年前向微软 Azure 累计⽀出 492 亿美元，较上⽉ S-1 ⽂
件披露的 449 亿美元增加约 43 亿美元；向⾕歌云的承诺⽀出则从 1145 亿美元升⾄ 1216 亿美元，
增幅约 71 亿美元。两项合计新增约 114 亿美元。
此前， Anthropic 在上⽉末提交的 S-1 ⽂件中已披露约 6500 亿美元的云计算总⽀出计划，其中包
括向博通承诺 1612 亿美元、向 SpaceX 承诺 1205 亿美元、向亚⻢逊承诺 1100 亿美元，以及与其
他云服务商签订的若⼲规模较⼩的协议。上述既有协议在周⼆⽂件中均维持不变。

Lambda 合同悬⽽未决，⼩型云服务商压⼒浮现
Anthropic 同时披露，与 Lambda 签订的 349 亿美元服务器租赁协议⾯临被取消的可能。 Anthropic
表⽰， Lambda 未能在双⽅约定的截⽌⽇期前完成相关数据中⼼的建设，这赋予了 Anthropic 依合
同解除协议的权利。不过，该公司亦表⽰ " ⽬前⽆意 " ⾏使这⼀权利。
这⼀表态揭⽰出中⼩型云服务商在快速扩张产能⽅⾯普遍⾯临的挑战 —— 在 AI 算⼒需求爆发式增
⻓的背景下，部分供应商难以在约定时间内完成基础设施部署。
短期合同成本拖累利润，算⼒需求仍待满⾜
Anthropic 在⽂件中警告，为满⾜不断攀升的算⼒需求，公司可能需要签订更多短期计算合同。
相较于⻓期协议，此类合同的成本 " ⼤幅偏⾼ " ，预计将在 " 近期 " 压缩公司的运营利润率。
财务数据印证了这⼀压⼒。 Anthropic 报告第三季度运营亏损 5.67 亿美元，⽽上⼀季度运营利润
为 9.74 亿美元，公司将这⼀骤变主要归因于短期算⼒合同的⾼额成本。

Document 495: インドネシア、ギブラン副大統領の辞任意向表明で政局に新たな波乱 ～プラボウォ・ジョコ両氏の関係変化を背景に、2029年に向けた_d1eb3c06

Source PDF: all report pdf/インドネシア、ギブラン副大統領の辞任意向表明で政局に新たな波乱 ～プラボウォ・ジョコ両氏の関係変化を背景に、2029年に向けた_d1eb3c06.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年11 月 11 日

1/5 
インドネシア、ギブラン副大統領の辞任意向表明で政局に新たな波乱 
～プラボウォ・ジョコ両氏の関係変化を背景に、2029 年に向けた政治勢力再編が進む可能性～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ インドネシアでは、ギブラン副大統領がプラボウォ大統領に辞任の意向を伝えたことが明ら
かになり、政局に新たな動揺が広がっている。背景には、2024 年の大統領選挙に際して提出
した学歴証明書類の有効性を巡り、司法機関が相次いで同氏に不利な判断を示したことがあ
る。議会でも資格問題を追及する動きが強まるなか、ギブラン氏は法的手段による対応を模
索する一方、問題が政権全体を巻き込む政治的混乱に発展することを回避すべく、辞任の意
向を示したと考えられる。ただし、現時点では正式な辞任には至っていない。 
○ 今回の問題の背景には、プラボウォ大統領とジョコ前大統領の政治的関係の変化が影響して
いる可能性もある。 両者は2024 年の大統領選挙を通じて協力関係を構築したものの、 プラボ
ウォ政権の発足後は、閣僚人事や新首都建設を巡る政策面での違いなどから、次第に距離が
生じてきたとされる。さらに、足元ではジョコ氏に近い人物の拘束や、同氏の次男が率いる
政党からの閣僚らの離党が相次ぎ、両者の政治的力学に変化が生じている可能性がある。 
○ ギブラン氏が正式に辞任した場合、後任の副大統領の選定を通じて、プラボウォ政権内部の
勢力関係が変化する可能性がある。幅広い政党による連立体制を背景に、辞任が直ちに政権
の存続を脅かすとは限らず、 むしろ後任人事を通じて政権基盤を強化する余地もある。 他方、
与党連立内部の利害調整が複雑化する可能性があるほか、ジョコ氏にとっては現政権への重
要な影響力行使の手段を失うことにつながるため、2029 年の次期大統領・議会選挙を見据え
た政治勢力の再編が進むことも考えられる。 
○ 一方、インドネシア金融市場では、中銀の独立性や財政規律への懸念などを背景に、通貨ル
ピアや株式相場への下押し圧力がくすぶっている。こうしたなかで政治的混乱が長期化すれ
ば、 政策運営の予見可能性に対する懸念を通じて金融市場の新たな重しとなる可能性がある。
先行きは、ギブラン氏の正式な辞任の有無に加え、プラボウォ政権がジョコ前政権からの政
治的な自立をどこまで進めるのか、そして 2029 年に向けて主要政治勢力の関係がどのよう
に変化するかが重要な焦点となる。

### Page 2

2/5 
【ギブラン副大統領が突然の辞任意向を表明、立候補資格を巡る問題の再燃が背景に】 
インドネシアの政局が新たな動揺に見舞われている。同国政府は 10 日、ギブラン副大統領がプ
ラボウォ大統領と約２時間半にわたって会談し、 副大統領職を辞任する意向を伝えたことを明らか
にした。政府側は、プラボウォ氏が辞任の意向を拒否しなかったと説明している。背景には、2024
年の大統領選挙に際して提出した学歴証明書類の有効性を巡り、 司法機関が相次いで同氏に不利な
判断を示したことがある。なお、ギブラン氏は辞任の意向を伝える一方、行政裁判所での手続きを
通じて自身の学歴証明書類の有効性を立証する機会を求めているとされる。 プラボウォ氏も法的手
段による対応を助言したとされ、現時点では正式な辞任には至っていない。このため、ギブラン氏
は引き続き副大統領として職務を遂行しているものの、 今回の動きは、2024 年の大統領選挙を通じ
て形成されたプラボウォ氏とジョコ前大統領の政治的協力関係に変化が生じている可能性を示唆
している。 
今回、 ギブラン氏による辞任意向表明に至った背景には、同氏の副大統領への立候補資格を巡る
問題がある。 同氏は、2024 年の大統領選挙への出馬に際して、選挙法で定める大統領、 副大統領候
補の年齢規定（40 歳以上）を下回る 36 歳であったにもかかわらず、憲法裁判所が地方自治体の首
長経験者について立候補年齢の例外を認めたことで、 副大統領候補となることが可能になった。 こ
の判断を巡っては、当時の憲法裁判所長官であったアンワル氏がジョコ前大統領の義弟（妹の夫）
に当たるなど利益相反が問題視され、 その後、 同氏は重大な倫理違反を理由に長官職を解かれた経
緯がある（注１）。しかし、 その後も司法判断そのものは無効とはされず、ギブラン氏はプラボウォ
氏とともに大統領選挙に勝利した。このため、同氏の立候補を巡っては、当初から政治的な論争が
続いてきたものの、足元では年齢要件とは別に、学歴要件を巡る問題が浮上している。 
具体的には、10 月６日に憲法裁判所が学歴要件を巡る申し立てを原告適格の問題から不受理と
した一方、ギブラン氏が海外（シンガポールのオーキッド・パーク中等学校、オーストラリアのシ
ドニー工科大学付属インサーチ） で受けた教育について、 国内の高校卒業資格に相当することを裏
付ける十分な証拠が確認できないとの見解を示した。さらに、10 月 30 日には最高裁判所が、2024
年の立候補に際して提出された高校卒業資格の同等性を認める証明書を無効とする判断を下した。
こうした司法判断を受けて、 議会でもギブラン氏の資格問題を追及する動きが強まっている。 国民
代表議会 （下院） では、 ６会派に所属する約50 人の議員が、 弾劾手続きにつながり得る 「意見表明
権」の行使を提案したとされる。このため、ギブラン氏としては、法的な争いを継続する姿勢を維
持する一方、 自身の進退問題が政権全体を巻き込む政治的混乱に発展することを回避すべく、 辞任
の意向を示したと考えられる。

【政局の底流には、プラボウォ大統領とジョコ前大統領の関係悪化懸念が影響している可能性も】 
今回の問題を巡っては、 ギブラン氏個人の立候補資格だけでは説明し切れない側面がある。 プラ

注１ 2023 年 11 月８日付レポート「インドネシア大統領選､ジョコ氏長男を｢後押し｣した憲法裁所長に辞任命令」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/288141.html）

### Page 3

3/5 
ボウォ氏とジョコ前大統領は、2014 年と 2019 年の大統領選挙では対立候補として激戦を演じた。
しかし、2019 年に発足したジョコ政権２期目では、 プラボウォ氏が国防相に登用されたことをきっ
かけに、 両者の関係が改善に転じた。さらに、2024 年の大統領選挙では、 プラボウォ氏がジョコ氏
の長男であるギブラン氏を副大統領候補に迎え、 ジョコ政権の政策を継承する姿勢を示したことで、
ジョコ氏の支持基盤を取り込むことに成功した。 このため、 プラボウォ政権の発足当初においては、
両者の政治的協力関係が政権基盤の安定化を図る重要な要素となっていた。 しかし、 政権発足から
時間が経過するにつれ、 プラボウォ氏が独自の政策運営を強める一方、内閣改造が実施されるたび
にジョコ前政権の関係者を要職から外す動きがみられた。 その結果、両者の関係には次第に距離が
生じてきたとされる。さらに、プラボウォ政権下では、ジョコ前大統領が推進した新首都ヌサンタ
ラの建設予算が抑制されるなど、政策面でも優先順位の違いが表面化している。 
足元では、両者の関係を一段と複雑する動きが顕在化している。国家警察は 11 月初め、ジョコ
氏に近いとされるリスティオ大統領前首席補佐官（前国家警察長官）らを、プラボウォ氏を含む政
府高官などへの違法な通信傍受に関与した疑いで拘束した。 この問題を巡っては捜査が続いており、
ジョコ氏などの関与が確認されている訳ではないものの、両者の政治的関係を巡る警戒感を高める
一因となっている。さらに、直後には、ジョコ氏の次男（カエサン氏）が党首を務めるインドネシ
ア連帯党（ＰＳＩ）に所属する現職閣僚や副大臣３名が相次いで離党を表明した。３名は離党の理
由に、プラボウォ政権での職務の専念などを挙げたものの、一連の動きは、政権関係者の間でジョ
コ氏との政治的な距離を見直す動きが生じている可能性を示唆している。その意味では、今回のギ
ブラン氏の辞任意向表明についても、こうした政治的な力学の変化を踏まえて捉える必要があ る。

【副大統領辞任なら政権基盤の再編が進む可能性、ジョコ前大統領の動きにも注意】 
今後、ギブラン氏が正式に副大統領職を辞任すれば、プラボウォ氏による政権運営にも一定の変
化が生じると考えられる。現行憲法では、副大統領に欠員が生じた場合、大統領が推薦する２人の
候補者から、国民協議会（国民代表議会と地方代表議会で構成）が 60 日以内に新たな副大統領を
選出すると定められている（第８条第２
項） 。このため、ギブラン氏の辞任後は、
後任 の選定を巡って与党 連立 内の調整
が必要となる。プラボウォ政権は幅広い
政党を取り込んだ連立体制を構築して
いるため （図１）、 副大統領の辞任が直ち
に政権の存続を脅かすとは限らない。 む
しろ、 後任人事を通じて大統領の政策運
営に協力的な勢力との結び付きを強め
ることも可能となる。ただし、主要政党
が後任ポストや政策面での影響力拡大
を求めれば、連立内部の利害調整がこれまで以上に複雑化することも予想される。加えて、司法判

4/5 
断や議会による追及への対応が長期化する場合には、 政権による政策遂行に対する政治的な負担が
高まることも考えられる。 
一方で、 今回の動きはジョコ前大統領の政治的な立場にも影響を及ぼすと考えられる。 同氏は大
統領退任後も高い知名度と一定の支持基盤を維持しており、 近年はカエサン氏が率いるＰＳＩを通
じて政治的な活動を活発化させてきた。しかし、ギブラン氏が副大統領を退任すれば、ジョコ氏が
現政権に対して影響力を行使するうえで重要な接点が失われる。 他方、 ジョコ氏が引き続きＰＳＩ
や自身の支持者組織を通じて政治活動を続ける場合、 政権との協力関係を再構築する動きが生じる
可能性があるものの、現政権とは異なる政治的な立場を鮮明にする展開も考えられる。とりわけ、
2029 年に実施予定の次期大統領 ・ 議会選挙に向けて、 プラボウォ氏を支える既存の与党勢力とジョ
コ氏に近い勢力がどのような関係を築くかは、 政治的な争点のひとつになる。 ジョコ氏の影響力の
行方を判断するうえでは、 ギブラン氏自身の政治活動の継続に加え、 ＰＳＩの組織基盤の拡大や既
存政党との協力関係が重要となる。

【政治的混乱が金融市場の新たな重しとなる可能性、2029 年に向けた政局再編にも注目】 
今回の政治的混乱によって、金融市場が大幅な動揺に見舞われるかは不透明である。とはいえ、
足元のインドネシア市場を巡っては、中銀の独立性を巡る懸念などを理由に通貨ルピア安圧力がく
すぶるとともに、指数算出会社のＭＳＣＩが同国を「スタンドアローン市場」に再分類する案につ
いて協議を開始したことなどを受け て

ルタ総合指数）は調整の動きを強めてい
る（図２） 。 さらに、プラボウォ政権が推
進する無償給食事業などの 大幅な財政
出動による悪影響への懸念も、市場の重
しとなっている。こうした状況のなかで
政治的な混乱が長期化すれば、政策運営
の予見可能性や財政規律に対する投資
家の評価を通じて、金融市場に影響が及
ぶ可能性は否定できない。とりわけ、与
党連立内部における対立が予算編成や政権による政策遂行に波及するか否かが重要になる。 
先行きについては、 ギブラン氏が司法手続きを通じて自身の立場を維持する場合と、 正式な辞任
に踏み切り、新たな副大統領の選出が進む場合の双方を念頭に置く必要がある。前者の場合は、資
格問題を巡る政治的な対立が長期化する可能性があり、 後者の場合は、 副大統領の後任人事を通じ
て政権内部の勢力関係が変化することが考えられる。ただし、いずれの展開となった場合でも、プ

Document 556: 今月のグラフ（2026年11月）銅価格、高値圏で推移

Source PDF: all report pdf/今月のグラフ（2026年11月）銅価格、高値圏で推移.pdf
Report source: MURC
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 11月 5 日

調査レポート

今月のグラフ（2026 年 11月）

銅価格、高値圏で推移

調査部 主任研究員 芥田知至

世界景気に敏感とされる銅価格が高値圏で推移している。国際指標となるロンドン金属取引所（LME）の 3 カ
月先物をみると、2026 年 9 月 10 日に 1 トンあたり 14,875 ドルと史上最高値を記録した。 
電気伝導性に優れる銅は、そもそも電気自動車や再生可能エネルギー向けの需要拡大に加えて、人工知能
（AI）・半導体に関連した需要増加も見込まれて、需給引き締まり観測が強まりやすかった。 
また、第 2 次トランプ政権の誕生後、米当局が銅製品などとともに銅地金の輸入にも、追加関税を賦課すると
の観測が浮上した。米国内で取引される銅の価格は関税分だけ高くなるとの思惑が働き、 COMEX（ニューヨー
ク商品取引所）の銅価格を含めて、米国内で取引される銅価格は、国際的な価格よりも高くなった。このため、各
地から銅地金が米国に輸入されて、米国外の銅需給を引き締め、LME 銅相場にも押し上げ要因になった。 
最高値をつけた9 月10 日に、銅地金への追加関税について、米政権がまだ検討中と報じられると、銅のLME
相場は一時下落したが、その後も関税賦課観測は根強く 14,000 ドル台を中心に推移し、高止まりしている。 
銅価格と米国の主要半導体関連企業で構成されるフィラデルフィア半導体株指数（ SOX）は、ともにエレクトロ
ニクス関連需要動向に敏感とみられ、連動性が強かったが、足元では SOX が下落気味なのに対して、銅価格は
高止まりしている。AI に対する期待の変動や長期金利高止まりのマイナス影響が SOX でより強く表れ、銅価格
は米関税賦課観測で支えられているためと思われる。

図表 銅価格（ LME）とフィラデルフィア半導体株指数（SOX）の推移

（出所） Bloomberg

歐洲製造業疲軟影響，管理層 現估明年需求成長有限， 中鋼出貨量

 1H26 精緻鋼品銷量佔比 11.5% ，但毛利佔比非常高。 未來將積極
拓展精緻鋼品銷售。  
 車用鋼多數為外銷，今年車市需求 已較去年 谷底回溫。  
 1Q-3Q26 外銷量約呈年持平 ；其中 ，歐洲受到進口防衛措施影響，
銷量減少 達 2/3 至 1/2，不足部分由韓國 、東南亞及墨西哥 等其他
市場補上 。 
 台灣央行升息對國內需求影響應有限。  
 中能風場 今年風機妥善率提升， 9 月開始風量漸增推動獲利 增加，
對比 2Q-3Q26 約落在損平邊緣  (台灣夏季風量較差 )。 
 中國累計前 9 月粗鋼產量年減 2.9% ，出口量 年減 2.6% 。

法說會重點摘要

智易 
(3596 TT, NT$ 137, 未評等)

重點摘要  
1. 3Q26 營收 146.4 億元，QoQ +5% 、YoY +6% ，毛利率 14.9% ，
季減 0.3ppts，費用率降至 8.0% 帶動營益率升至 6.9% ，EPS 3.46
元與 1-9M26 EPS 9.96 元，皆創歷史新高。為因應記憶體漲價，公
司策略性備料， 3Q26 底存貨 393.8 億元、約一半為記憶體，短期
借款升至 86.9 億元 
2. 公司對 2027 年看法為「樂觀審慎」。北美 Tier 1 電信商與 MSO
新客戶將於 2027 年分別出貨 PON 與 Cable Modem， Broadband 
YoY 雙位數以上成長無虞； 5G FWA 穩定成長；企業端 Switch 有
3-4 家美系新客戶出貨，企業端占比目標由 2026 年低於 5%提升
至雙位數 
3. Wi-Fi 8 與 50G PON 專案於 2028 年陸續出貨，衛星代工、車用與
Edge AI 為 2028 年接棒動能。我們認為北美新客戶機種生命週期
可延續至 2028- 2029 年，加上企業端低基期放量， 2027- 2028 年
成長具延續性； 2027 年記憶體需求約 9 成已備齊，可降低記憶體
續漲對毛利率的衝擊  
結論 
我們看好智易 2027 年在北美電信與 MSO 新客戶同步放量、企業端
Switch 低基期翻倍成長帶動下，營收成長重回加速軌道，惟 主晶片與
PCB 等成本上漲仍為 2027 年毛利率主要壓力，公司以營益率維持 6.5-
7%為目標，後續驗證點為漲價轉嫁進度與北美新客戶出貨節奏；中長
期 Wi-Fi 8 、50G PON 升級與衛星、車用、Edge A I 布局發酵，可望使
成長延續至 2028 年

3Q26 營運與財務概況  
 3Q26 營收 146.4 億元，QoQ +5% 、YoY +6% ；營業毛利 21.9 億
元，毛利率 14.9% ，低於 2Q26 的 15.2% 與 3Q25 的 15.3%  
 3Q26 費用率 8.0% ，低於 2Q26 的 8.5% ；營業淨利 10.1 億元，
QoQ +9% 、YoY +13% ，營益率 6.9% ，QoQ +0.2ppts ；本期淨
利 7.6 億元，EPS 3.46 元，創單季歷史新高  
 1-9M26 營收 413.1 億元， YoY +3% ，毛利率 15.1% ，營益率
6.7% 與 1-9M25 持平；本期淨利 21.9 億元，YoY +5% ，EPS 9.96
元，同創歷史新高  
 3Q26 產品組合： 
 行動通訊 42%（2Q26 38% ），主因 5G FWA 銷售較佳 
 智慧家庭 29%（2Q26 32% ），主因 Wi-Fi Extender 銷量較低 
 寬頻固網 27%（2Q26 26% ）

 3Q26 區域組合：美洲 60%仍為最大營收來源，歐洲 28% ，亞洲
及其他 12%（2Q26 10% ） ，為本季成長最多的區域

記憶體策略性備料與零組件漲價  
 3Q26 底存貨 393.8 億元，占總資產 64%（2Q26 底 51%、3Q25
底 31% ） ，其中接近 200 億元為記憶體；短期借款 86.9 億元，
3Q25 底為 0，全數用於記憶體備料；每股淨值 78.11 元 
 2027 年所需記憶體約 9 成已備齊，是記憶體漲價期間毛利率得以
維持穩定的重要因素  
 現金流轉負主因囤積記憶體；若記憶體價格反轉、或決定不再為
2028 年積極備料，出貨後即可償還借款、回到現金充裕狀態  
 漲價不只記憶體：  
 主晶片自 4Q26 開始漲價，漲幅接近兩位數、約 1 成 
 PCB 漲價且交期延長

Broadband ：北美新客戶 2027 年同步放量  
 AI 應用帶動企業與家用網通在連線穩定度、頻寬與延遲上同步升
級，升級節奏由 Wi-Fi 6 走向 Wi-Fi 7 ；Wi-Fi 8 與光纖 10G 升至
50G PON 的專案已於 2026 年開案，2028 年陸續出貨 
 2026 年 Wi-Fi 7 出貨比重約 5 成多、接近 6 成，2027 年有機會達
8 成左右，2H27 會有 Wi-Fi 8 
 北美耕耘多年，2027 年北美大型電信商與 MSO 業者將同步出貨，
含新客戶與新專案，新增客戶皆為北美 Tier 1 ，且皆為客戶的重要
機種： 
 電信商出貨 PON 產品 
 MSO 業者出貨 Cable Modem ，2027 年新導入北美大型 MSO 客
戶 
 Broadband 為 2027 年最主要成長來源，該產品線 YoY 雙位數以上
成長無虞；機種生命週期長， 2027 年出貨產品部分可賣到 2029 年
才換代 
 策略為提高 Tier 1 大客戶的產品滲透率，同一客戶除 FWA 外還有
PON、Gateway Router 、Extender 等產品，目標在既有客戶身上多
拿機種

5G FWA ：穩定成長  
 3Q26 行動通訊不論占比或金額成長幅度皆較大，美國電信商在既
有 5G 基礎建設上搶市占， FWA 訂閱用戶數近幾季快速成長 
 2027 年 5G FWA 成長幅度不會像 Broadband 那麼大，定調為穩定
成長；歐洲量體較小，但同樣逐步成長

### Page 3

台灣 智易

十一月 10, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 3

Enterprise ：資安客戶穩健、 Switch 新客戶翻倍成長  
 美系資安客戶為企業端量最大的客戶， 2027 年給的案子越來越
多，訂單量逐步上升；公司負責硬體與整合，不碰軟體 
 Switch 2026 年僅樣品出貨，第一個新客戶自 2026 年底開始量產
出貨；2026 年底至 2027 年將有 3-4 家美國新客戶出貨，2027 年
Switch 營收翻倍以上成長  
 Switch 主攻 400G 以下市場，包括政府機關、TAA （美國政府標
案） 、機構與中小企業需求，公司手上已有 TAA 案 
 企業端占比 2026 年低於 5%，2027 年目標突破兩位數

衛星、車用與 Edge AI 布局 
 衛星：近期多家客戶來公司與工廠參訪，首要目標為以工廠能力承
接代工，2027 年有機會拿下代工案但貢獻不大  
 車用：由商用車 ADAS 轉為與母公司仁寶車用部門整合，以仁寶紅
外線相機模組搭配公司雷達做長距離輔助，日系車廠已有案子給仁
寶；2027 年有機會進入 Trial Run 、出 sample 量，2028 年放量，
目標轉向一般轎車前裝市場 
 Edge AI ：除既有電信商外，高齡照護、資安防護，以及賣場、餐
廳等場域客戶主動洽談，1 -2 家已在場域測試，第一個產品預計
2H27 進入商轉

QA 
 4Q26 營運展望？ 
 記憶體持續漲價，毛利率仍有壓力，努力維持在目前水準 
 營收小幅下滑 low single digit ，各產品都只掉一點  
 費用率與 3Q26 的 8.0% 持平或略低  
 2027 年全公司營收能否達雙位數成長？  
 一定比 2026 年好，2027-2028 年 YoY 成長相較 2025- 2026 年
都會增加 
 2027 年毛利率展望？  
 客戶在記憶體漲價一波已受傷，仍自行吸收成本；晶片自 4Q26
開始漲價，若未能轉嫁，1Q27 毛利率就會受影響  
 公司已準備與客戶協商，2027 年情況不至於很嚴重，但組裝廠
毛利率一定會有壓力，目標努力維持 3Q-4Q26 水準 
 各產品線毛利率高低？  
 Mobility 最好，其次 Broadband， Smart Home 受 Set-top Box 拖
累，記憶體漲價後 Set-top Box 因記憶體用量大、毛利率大幅下
滑 
 2027 年產品線與區域成長排序？ 
 產品線：Broadband > Mobil ity，Broadband 主要來自美國與歐
洲光纖升級，5G FWA 則為穩定成長

### Page 4

台灣 智易

十一月 10, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 4

 區域：美洲為最主要成長來源，歐洲勝過亞洲；亞洲競爭激烈，
搶單對毛利不一定是好事  
 Smart Home 下滑原因？  
 Set-top Box 下滑最明顯，占比已 降至約 5%；Extender 自 3Q26
起轉弱，主要是 1-2 個客戶出貨量下滑  
 Extender 並非必要設備，不像 PON、Cable Modem 每戶一定要
裝一台 
 3Q26 亞洲比重上升的原因？ 
 南亞客戶受記憶體影響拉貨仍放緩， 3Q26 成長主要來自東北亞
客戶的 Wireless Module ，2026 年出貨穩定增加  
 印度、南亞 2026 年跌幅大， 2027 年客戶庫存去化後仍需出新
產品，預期較 2026 年復甦 
 5G FWA 是否北美三大電信商都是客戶？  
 目前為 2 家，努力爭取第 3 家 
 2027 年費用展望？  
 新竹二期工程已完工，主要作為研發與實驗室，2026 年底開始
提列折舊；車用、衛星、Edge AI 擴編研發人力，管銷研費用都
會增加 
 希望在營收成長帶動下費用率持平或下降，維持營益率 6.5-7% 
 股利政策？  
 過往配發率約 60-70%，2027 年發放時程原則上在 4 月底，以
6 成配發率計算約 7-8 元 
 南亞市場價格競爭？  
 一個案子十幾家台廠競標，價格壓力嚴峻，公司會挑案子，不會
每案都搶
